1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Menu, Search } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	
4	defineProps<{ collapsed: boolean }>()
5	const emit = defineEmits<{ "toggle-sidebar": [] }>()
6	
7	const { t } = useI18n()
8	const route = useRoute()
9	const router = useRouter()
10	const localePath = useLocalePath()
11	
12	const q = ref<string>(typeof route.query.q === "string" ? route.query.q : "")
13	
14	watch(
15	  () => route.query.q,
16	  (next) => {
17	    q.value = typeof next === "string" ? next : ""
18	  },
19	)
20	
21	function onSubmit() {
22	  const query: Record<string, string> = {}
23	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
24	    if (typeof v === "string" && k !== "q" && k !== "page") query[k] = v
25	  }
26	  const trimmed = q.value.trim()
27	  if (trimmed) query.q = trimmed
28	  router.push({ path: localePath("/extensions"), query })
29	}
30	</script>
31	
32	<template>
33	  <div class="contents">
34	    <button
35	      type="button"

50	
51	    <form
52	      role="search"

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import {
3	  Boxes,
4	  ChevronDown,
5	  ChevronRight,
6	  Command,
7	  Folder,
8	  Globe2,
9	  Plug,
10	  Plus,
11	  Zap,
12	} from "lucide-vue-next"
13	import { FUNC_CAT_COLORS, FUNC_TAXONOMY } from "~~/shared/taxonomy"
14	import type { Component } from "vue"
15	
16	defineProps<{ collapsed: boolean }>()
17	
18	const { t } = useI18n()
19	const route = useRoute()
20	const localePath = useLocalePath()
21	
22	type Category = "all" | "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
23	
24	const BROWSE_ITEMS: { key: Category; labelKey: string; Icon: Component }[] = [
25	  { key: "all", labelKey: "sidebar.allExtensions", Icon: Boxes },
26	  { key: "skills", labelKey: "sidebar.skills", Icon: Zap },
27	  { key: "mcp", labelKey: "sidebar.mcpServers", Icon: Globe2 },
28	  { key: "slash", labelKey: "sidebar.slashCommands", Icon: Command },
29	  { key: "plugins", labelKey: "sidebar.plugins", Icon: Plug },
30	]
31	
32	const COLLECTION_PLACEHOLDERS = [
33	  { id: "p1", name: "My Tools" },
34	  { id: "p2", name: "Reviewed" },
35	]
36	
37	const activeCategory = computed<Category>(() => {
38	  const raw = route.query.category
39	  if (typeof raw === "string" && ["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"].includes(raw)) {
40	    return raw as Category
41	  }
42	  return "all"
43	})
44	
45	const activeFuncCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.funcCat === "string" ? route.query.funcCat : null))
46	const activeSubCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.subCat === "string" ? route.query.subCat : null))
47	const activeL2 = computed(() => (typeof route.query.l2 === "string" ? route.query.l2 : null))
48	
49	const expansion = reactive({
50	  sections: { browse: true, categories: true, collections: false },
51	  funcCats: {} as Record<string, boolean>,
52	  l1: {} as Record<string, boolean>,
53	})
54	
55	if (activeFuncCat.value) expansion.funcCats[activeFuncCat.value] = true
56	else expansion.funcCats.workTask = true
57	if (activeSubCat.value) expansion.l1[activeSubCat.value] = true
58	
59	watch(activeFuncCat, (next) => {
60	  if (next) expansion.funcCats[next] = true
61	})
62	watch(activeSubCat, (next) => {
63	  if (next) expansion.l1[next] = true
64	})
65	
66	function buildHref(updates: Record<string, string | null>): string {
67	  const params = new URLSearchParams()
68	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
69	    if (typeof v === "string" && k !== "page") params.set(k, v)
70	  }
71	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(updates)) {
72	    if (v === null) params.delete(k)
73	    else params.set(k, v)
74	  }
75	  const qs = params.toString()
76	  const path = localePath("/extensions")
77	  return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path
78	}
79	
80	function toggleSection(key: "browse" | "categories" | "collections") {
81	  expansion.sections[key] = !expansion.sections[key]
82	}
83	function toggleFuncCat(key: string) {
84	  expansion.funcCats[key] = !expansion.funcCats[key]
85	}
86	function toggleL1(key: string) {
87	  expansion.l1[key] = !expansion.l1[key]
88	}
89	</script>
90	
91	<template>
92	  <div v-if="!collapsed" class="min-w-[200px] flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 text-(--color-ink)">
93	    <!-- Browse section -->
94	    <div class="mb-1">
95	      <button
96	        type="button"
97	        class="flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)"

99	        @click="toggleSection('browse')"

103	        <ChevronRight v-else :size="14" aria-hidden="true" />

> AGENT

I've read the layout (`app/layouts/default.vue`, `TopBar`, `Sidebar`, home page, and component inventory). The bones are good — editorial palette, Fraunces + JetBrains Mono, a sticky 52px top bar, a 240px sidebar, semantic CSS variables. The "elegance gap" is mostly **information density and visual rhythm**, not the look-and-feel system itself.

The four moves I'd weigh, in rough priority order:

**1. Top bar is overloaded.** Right now it carries hamburger + brand + search + 4 nav links + theme + locale + user = ~9 affordances at 52px tall. That's why it reads as "utility chrome" instead of an editorial header. Two ways to fix:
- *Aggressive*: drop the inline nav (Explore / MCP Panorama / Publish / Docs) into the sidebar, leaving the top bar as **brand · search · identity**. Cleaner, but Publish/Docs become one click deeper.
- *Conservative*: keep nav but make the bar 64px tall, give the brand more breathing room, and switch search to a quieter ghost style that expands on focus.

**2. The home hero is generic.** `app/pages/index.vue:26-45` is a 100%-width gradient card whose payload is just the word "AgentCenter" and "View extension". Editorial homes earn their fold by *featuring a specific thing* — a hand-picked extension of the week with its mark, author, tagline, and install line. Same component footprint, infinitely more elegant.

**3. Sidebar has three competing sections.** Browse + Categories (3 levels deep) + Collections in a 240px rail is a lot of UI for "elegant". I'd consider moving the taxonomy tree (`FUNC_TAXONOMY`) into the filter drawer on `/extensions` and keeping the sidebar to **type pills + collections only**. The taxonomy is mostly relevant *while filtering*, not as global navigation.

**4. Use the accent more sparingly.** The accent appears on the hero badge, active sidebar items, active nav, hero CTA, dots, rules — basically every viewport has 6–10 accent hits. Restraint here (1–2 per screen) is the single biggest "feels expensive" move on an editorial site.

My recommendation if you only do one: **#2 (real featured extension on the home hero)** — highest visible payoff, low risk, doesn't disturb routing or info architecture. Tradeoff: you need an editorial "featured" flag (a column, a cookie pin, or a hand-curated query) and a fallback when nothing is featured.

Want me to sketch any of these as a quick prototype, or talk through one in more depth before touching code?

## TopBar/Header Layout Refactor — Exploration Report

### Key Findings

**I18n Keys for Inline Nav (TopBar.vue:71-100)**
- `nav.explore` (Explore → /extensions)
- `nav.mcpPanorama` (MCP Panorama → /mcp-panorama)
- `nav.publish` (Publish → /publish)
- `nav.docs` (Docs) — rendered as disabled `<span>` with `aria-disabled="true"` and title tooltip pointing to `nav.comingSoon`
- Hidden on mobile (`hidden md:flex`)
- Uses `active-class="bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent)"` for route highlighting

**Search Input (TopBar.vue:51-69)**
- Owns focus state via `q` ref tracking route.query.q (lines 12-19)
- Watchers sync query param bidirectionally
- Form-based submission via `onSubmit()` pushes `/extensions` with query params preserved
- No explicit focus-expand animation; only ring-2 on `:focus` (line 67)
- Placeholder: `search.placeholder`

**Sidebar Readiness (Sidebar.vue:93-250)**
- Opens with **Browse section** (lines 94-117): 5 category links (All/Skills/MCP/Slash/Plugins)
- Divider line (line 119), then **Categories section** (lines 122-208) with taxonomy drill-down
- Divider, then **Collections section** (placeholder, disabled)
- **No obvious primary nav slot before Browse** — moving Explore/Publish/Docs there would require inserting a new section or repurposing the top of Browse
- Browse items all link to `/extensions?category=X`, Categories is deeper taxonomy
- Sidebar is already dense; inserting 4 nav links at top would work but competes with Browse structure

**A11y Patterns (Both layouts & TopBar)**
1. **Skip-link** (`app/layouts/default.vue:11-16` & `mcp-panorama.vue:14-19`): `sr-only` link to `#main-content` with visible on `:focus`, styled with focus ring — **must preserve**
2. **Focus rings**: TopBar menu button (line 36) and search input (line 67) both use `focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)` — **consistent pattern**
3. **ARIA attributes**:
   - TopBar menu: `aria-label`, `aria-expanded` (line 37-38)
   - Search: `role="search"`, label via `<label class="sr-only">` (lines 52, 56)
   - Sidebar: `role="navigation"`, section buttons use `aria-expanded` + `aria-label` for expand/collapse
   - Docs span: `aria-disabled="true"` (line 96)
4. **No aria-current** in TopBar nav — uses `active-class` styling only. Sidebar category links also use styling only, not `aria-current="page"`

**MCP-Panorama Layout Differences (mcp-panorama.vue)**
- **Identical header** (lines 21-26, same as default.vue 18-23)
- **No global Sidebar** — uses `sidebarCollapsed = true` permanently (line 6)
- **Page renders custom LayerSidebar** inside `<main>` (documented in comment, lines 3-5)
- Purpose: Let MCP Panorama page own its sidebar state and appearance without global nav competing
- Single structural change: omit `<aside>` block, so page has full flex space

### CSS Variables (tailwind.css:6-32, dark/mono overrides below)
Core variables you'll preserve when increasing topbar height:
- `--color-bg`, `--color-card` (backgrounds)
- `--color-sidebar` (hover states)
- `--color-accent`, `--color-accent-fg` (active/focus)
- `--color-border`, `--color-ink`, `--color-ink-muted` (text/dividers)
- `--radius-card: 10px` (for search input)

**Dark & Mono themes** have full override sets (lines 34-78); refactor must test all three.

### Tests
- **Sidebar.nuxt.test.ts** (2296 bytes): Checks section headers (Browse/Categories/Collections), collapsed behavior, taxonomy rendering. **No TopBar tests found** — you can add focus behavior tests when implementing search expansion.

### Suggested Refactor Flow
1. Move nav links OUT: Delete TopBar lines 71-100 (`<nav>` block)
2. Add nav section to Sidebar TOP: Insert before Browse section (before line 94), as collapsible "Marketplace" or flat list
3. Increase TopBar height: Current `h-[52px]` (line 19, default.vue) → test `h-[64px]` or `h-[72px]`; adjust padding/gaps (line 19: `px-4 gap-3`)
4. Make search "ghost": Remove border, use placeholder background, expand on focus — modify line 67 classes
5. Test a11y: Focus order, skip-link still works, new Sidebar section has aria-expanded, search focus visible

**No i18n additions needed** — reuse existing `nav.explore`, `nav.publish`, `nav.mcpPanorama`, `nav.docs` keys.

## Exploration Report: Featured Extension Mechanism

### Current State: NO Built-In Featured Mechanism

**Schema has no featured flag.** The extensions table (`shared/db/schema/extension.ts` lines 71–134) includes:
- `badge` enum (official/popular/new) — editorial marks, but no "featured" or "pinned" column
- No `featured`, `homePick`, `editorial`, or similar boolean

**Existing `findFeatured()` function** (`server/repositories/extensions.ts` lines 121–131) already exists but:
- Queries by **top download count** (`ORDER BY downloadsCount DESC LIMIT 1`)
- Returns a single extension matching `ExtensionListItem` shape
- Not wired into any API endpoint yet

### What's Already Available for Reuse

**Data shape for a featured hero** (`shared/db/queries-types.ts` lines 8–27):
- `ExtensionListItem` has: id, slug, category, badge, deptId, iconEmoji, iconColor, name, nameZh, description, descriptionZh, downloadsCount, starsAvg, tagIds
- Missing from list select: `tagline`, `taglineZh` (available in `detailSelect` at lines 59–60)

**Visual components**:
- `ExtCard.vue` (lines 20–27): localizes name/description via `nameZh`/`descriptionZh`; shows badge, dept, tags, star/download counts
- `ExtHero.vue` (lines 43–54): larger icon + h1 title + description + star/download metrics; already used on detail pages
- `InstallButton.vue`: reusable install affordance with loading/installed states

**Reference pattern**: `McpPanoramaFeature.vue` (lines 40–49) shows visual rhythm for a featured editorial section — eyebrow label, h2 title, description, stats, CTA.

**i18n keys exist** (`i18n/locales/en.json` lines 30–37):
- `home.featuredLabel`: "Featured this week"
- `home.featuredDescription`, `home.trendingTitle`, `home.browseAll`, `home.viewExtension`, `home.installsCount`
- Schema supports Chinese via `nameZh`, `descriptionZh`, `taglineZh` columns

### API/Query Layer

**extensions.get.ts** (lines 7–10): calls `extensionsRepo.findManyForList(db, filters)` with sort param `downloads`
- Uses `parseFilters()` from `shared/validators/filters.ts`
- Filters enum includes: `sort: ["downloads", "stars", "recent"]`; no "featured" sort yet

**Query builder** (`shared/search/query.ts` lines 19–47): `buildExtensionWhere()` enforces `visibility == published` globally; no featured-specific clauses.

### Minimum Viable Path

1. **Add `featured` boolean to schema** (`shared/db/schema/extension.ts`):
   - `featured: boolean().notNull().default(false)` at line ~105
   - Add index: `index("idx_ext_featured").on(t.featured)`

2. **Update `listSelect`** to include `tagline`/`taglineZh` (`server/repositories/extensions.ts` lines 24–43)

3. **Create `/api/internal/featured` endpoint** that calls `extensionsRepo.findFeatured(db)` or adds a new `featured == true` query variant

4. **Create `ExtFeatureHero.vue`** component combining ExtHero proportions with McpPanoramaFeature rhythm (eyebrow, h2, multi-line description, mark/tags, install button, department trail)

5. **Wire to home**: fetch `/api/internal/featured`, pass to `ExtFeatureHero`, position above `McpPanoramaFeature` at `index.vue` line 47

### File Paths for Implementation

| What | Path | Key Lines |
|------|------|-----------|
| Home page | `/app/pages/index.vue` | 7–10 (fetch), 47 (McpPanoramaFeature placement) |
| Extension schema | `/shared/db/schema/extension.ts` | 71–134 (table definition) |
| Featured query (stub) | `/server/repositories/extensions.ts` | 121–131 |
| List item type | `/shared/db/queries-types.ts` | 8–27 |
| Reference visual component | `/app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.vue` | 40–49 (layout pattern) |
| Hero variant (reference) | `/app/components/extension/ExtHero.vue` | Entire file |
| Install button | `/app/components/extension/InstallButton.vue` | Entire file |
| i18n keys | `/i18n/locales/en.json` | 30–37 |
| Filters validator | `/shared/validators/filters.ts` | 1–31 |
| Query builder | `/shared/search/query.ts` | 19–47 |

**Key decision**: Hand-curated `featured = true` column is simpler than an env var or constant list, and composes cleanly with the existing badge system. Admin UI can toggle one checkbox per extension; no sorting logic needed.

## PART A: Filter Drawer Architecture for Taxonomy

**FilterBar structure (reading from `/extensions/index.vue`):**
- `FilterBar` is an inline component (not a drawer), composed of pipeable filter widgets
- Uses a Popover pattern for expandable pickers (DeptPicker, CreatorPicker, PublisherPicker)
- TagDrawer is a nested Popover-based component (button → PopoverContent)
- **Already extensible:** Add a new "FuncCategoryPicker" alongside DeptPicker in the same layout

**URL state pattern:**
- DeptPicker writes/reads `filters.value.dept` via `useFilters()` composable → updates URL as `?dept=...`
- Sidebar currently writes `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2` query params via `buildHref()` (lines 66–78)
- **Action:** Move sidebar's `buildHref` logic to a filter composable; DeptPicker already demonstrates the pattern (reactive state → update() call → URL sync)

**L2/L3 expansion pattern in DeptPicker (YES—you can mirror this):**
- Lines 25–40: `expandedPath` computed prop tracks which dept nodes should be open based on active filter
- Lines 36–40: watches route changes, syncs expansion state
- Lines 56–63: `flatten()` recursively renders visible tree with chevrons for expand/collapse
- **The pattern is ideal for `FUNC_TAXONOMY`:** Use same computed + flatten() approach for categories → L1 → L2

---

## PART B: Accent Color Audit

**Total usage: 113 lines across 61 files** — concentrated in 4 hotspots:

| Surface | Count | Hottest Files |
|---------|-------|---|
| **Layout** | 18 | Sidebar (6), TopBar (5), ThemeSwitch (3), UserButton (3) |
| **Filters** | 19 | TagDrawer (5), DeptPicker (5), CreatorPicker (3), PublisherPicker (3), FilterChips (1), ScopePills (1), SortSelect (1) |
| **Extension** | 13 | ExtCard (3), InstallButton (1), SaveButton (1), ExtAboutCard (1), ExtTabs (1), ExtGrid (1), ExtRelatedList (1), McpPanoramaFeature (1), others (2) |
| **Auth/Publish** | 19 | sign-up (5), sign-in (4), cli/auth (4), publish pages (2), onboard (1), error.vue (1) |
| **UI primitives** | 12 | Button (3), Checkbox (2), Input (1), SelectTrigger (1), Textarea (1), DialogContent (1), SheetContent (1), TabsTrigger (1), TabsContent (1) |
| **Publish wizard** | 13 | WizardListing (3), PubChoice (3), others (7) |
| **MCP Panorama** | 9 | LayerSidebar (4), ToolDetailPanel (3), StatusChip (1), ToolCard (1) |
| **Profile** | 5 | ProfileHero (1), SectionDrafts (2), SectionPublished (1), SectionSaved (1) |
| **Home/Extensions pages** | 5 | index.vue (3), mcp-panorama.vue (1), extensions/index.vue (1) |

**Dial-back candidates:**
- **Sidebar:** 6 uses for active states (bg + text) in tree navigation—could reduce to border-left accent + neutral text
- **TopBar:** 5 uses mixed (5 cases for ring focus states + text)—focus rings at `/30–/40` opacity are appropriate; text underuse OK
- **Filters:** Heavy on active pills (bg-accent + text-accent-fg)—cohesive but could try outline style for secondary states
- **Auth pages:** 5–5 gradient/button clusters—unavoidable; these are CTA-heavy landing flows

# Phased Refactor Plan — AgentCenter Elegance Pass

## 0. mcp-panorama nav resolution (blocks A)

**Recommendation: option (a) with a twist — drop the layout's forced collapse and let the page own its sidebar state.** The current `mcp-panorama.vue` sets `sidebarCollapsed = true` because the page wanted to render its own `LayerSidebar`. After Phase A the global sidebar carries primary nav, so removing it strands users on `/mcp-panorama` with no way back to Explore/Publish. Options (b) Sheet and (c) icon-rail both cost a new component and a second nav surface to maintain. (a) is the cheapest correct answer: start collapsed (preserve current layout), but allow toggling via the existing TopBar menu button — which already works. The page-owned `LayerSidebar` renders alongside the main content area, not in the global aside slot, so they don't conflict. Net change: delete the forced `true` in `mcp-panorama.vue:6` and let the default `false` apply, OR keep `true` default but ensure the menu button is wired (it already is — line 25). Verify: route to `/mcp-panorama`, click menu, primary nav appears.

## 1. Final ordering: **B → A → C → D**

Rationale:
- **B first** — DB migration is the only irreversible move. Land it before anyone is depending on tints/layouts that touch the home hero. Migration risk lives alone in its own commit so a rollback doesn't drag UX work with it.
- **A second** — slim TopBar + sidebar nav section. Resolves the mcp-panorama block. Independent of B's data shape (hero already renders before A lands).
- **C third** — sidebar Categories deletion only makes sense after A has added the primary-nav section (otherwise an intermediate commit leaves the sidebar with only Browse + Collections, which is fine but feels naked). Also gives users `FuncCategoryPicker` parity before removing the drill-down.
- **D last** — accent restraint touches surfaces B/A/C also touch. Doing D twice = waste. Plus D is the lowest-risk, easiest-revert phase, so it makes a clean tail.

## 2. Per-phase detail

### Phase B — Featured hero (lands first)

**Touched / created:**
- `shared/db/schema/extension.ts` — add `featured: boolean().notNull().default(false)` after `visibility` (line ~93). New index: `idx_ext_featured_published` on `(featured, publishedAt DESC)` for the lookup.
- Generate via `bun drizzle-kit generate` → new `drizzle/000X_*.sql`.
- `server/repositories/extensions.ts` — extend `listSelect` (add `tagline`, `taglineZh`); rewrite `findFeatured` to `where featured=true AND visibility='published' order by published_at desc limit 1`, with a fallback `select` to top-downloaded if no row returns.
- New `server/api/internal/featured.get.ts` — thin wrapper returning `{ featured: ExtListItem | null }`.
- New `app/components/extension/ExtFeatureHero.vue` — reuses `ExtHero.vue` typography rhythm + `InstallCommand.vue`; new file but composed from existing primitives. Eyebrow from `home.featuredLabel` (already in i18n).
- `app/pages/index.vue` — replace lines 26-45 with `<ExtFeatureHero v-if="featured" :extension="featured" /> <FallbackHero v-else />` (extract current block as inline fallback or keep generic). Adds second `useFetch` for `/api/internal/featured`.

**Reused:** `ExtHero` rhythm, `InstallCommand`, `home.featuredLabel`, `listSelect` shape (extended).
**Created:** column, migration, endpoint, `ExtFeatureHero.vue`.
**Risks:** Migration on a populated DB needs `default false` (done) — safe. `findFeatured` consumers exist (home, possibly fixtures); adding columns to `listSelect` may break tests that snapshot the result shape — verify integration tests under `tests/integration`. Also: `tagline` is currently nullable in schema — ensure the hero handles null gracefully.
**Verification:** `bun run db:migrate && bun run dev` → home renders fallback; manually `UPDATE extensions SET featured=true WHERE slug='...'` → home renders new hero.

### Phase A — TopBar slim-down + Sidebar primary nav

Reuse the existing section/link pattern; pull labels from existing i18n keys `nav.explore`, `nav.mcpPanorama`, `nav.publish`, `nav.docs`. Add `Compass`, `Map`, `Upload`, `BookOpen` lucide icons (icons aren't tokens).

New nav links inflate the `>= 5 links` assertion (line 30) — still passes, just more.

Sidebar/secondary-filter active states use neutral ink + left-rule, not tinted fills." Plus mention in `docs/plan.md` if there's a UI conventions section.

**Created:** nothing.

Grep first. Theme drift: triple-check dark + Mono Clean themes — the left-rule contrast against `--color-sidebar` should still read.
**Verification:** screenshot diff across 3 themes; eyeball the 113-uses-down-to-target reduction (target ~60 uses).

## 3. Test strategy

**Existing files needing updates:**
- `app/components/layout/Sidebar.nuxt.test.ts` — heavy rewrite. After Phase A: assertions for primary-nav links (Explore/MCP/Publish/Docs); after Phase C: drop Categories/funcCat assertions. Worth doing in two commits matching A and C so the test history mirrors the code.

**New tests:**
- `app/components/extension/ExtFeatureHero.nuxt.test.ts` (Phase B) — renders eyebrow, name, tagline (en+zh fallback), install command line; null-safe when tagline missing.
- `app/components/filters/FuncCategoryPicker.nuxt.test.ts` (Phase C) — opens popover, displays funcCat → l1 → l2 hierarchy, selecting an l2 calls `update({ funcCat, subCat, l2 })` with the right triple. Stub `useFilters`.
- `server/repositories/extensions.featured.test.ts` (integration, Phase B) — fixture inserts one `featured=true`, two top-downloaded; assert `findFeatured` returns the featured row, not the top-downloaded; assert fallback when no featured.
- Contract test for `/api/internal/featured.get.ts` — `tests/contract/internal-featured.test.ts` returning `{ featured: ExtListItem | null }`.

**Skip:** E2E. Pure visual changes — Playwright would be noise unless the team already has a visual diff harness (none in `playwright.config.ts` based on the typical setup).

## 4. Commits

Sub-commits per phase, scoped Conventional Commits per CLAUDE.md §43:

- **B**: `feat(p?-db): add featured column to extensions` + migration | `feat(p?-repo): findFeatured prefers featured rows` | `feat(p?-ui): featured hero on home`
- **A**: `refactor(p?-layout): move primary nav from topbar to sidebar` (includes header height, ghost search, mcp-panorama unforce, sidebar tests update for nav)
- **C**: `feat(p?-filters): FuncCategoryPicker component` | `refactor(p?-layout): remove categories section from sidebar` (test rewrite goes here)
- **D**: `refactor(p?-ui): restrain accent usage on secondary surfaces` | `docs: lock accent-usage convention`

Phase scope token (`p4-elegance`, `p5-elegance`, whatever the current phase number is) — match `docs/plan.md` numbering.

## 5. Landmines & conflicts

- **`useFilters` may not own `funcCat`/`subCat`/`l2`** — sidebar wrote URL params directly via `buildHref`. Phase C must verify this and extend `shared/validators/filters.ts` if needed. Check this *before* starting C, not during.
- **Sticky-positioned children** of `default.vue` using `top-[52px]` will float wrong after header grows to 64px. `grep -r "top-\[52px\]" app/`.
- **`McpPanoramaFeature` on home** (`index.vue:47`) — still renders below the new featured hero. If it carries its own accent gradient, you've now stacked two accent moments. Audit before D.
- **Locked decision #2** (themes) is safe — no new tokens. **Locked decision #3** (Mode B drawer filters) is safe — `FuncCategoryPicker` follows the same Popover pattern. No conflicts.
- **CLI/API contract** — `featured` column is not exposed via `/api/v1`, so the frozen contract holds. Confirm `server/api/v1/*` doesn't accidentally return the new column via spread.
- **i18n** — `nav.docs` is currently "coming soon" (disabled span). When moving to sidebar, decide: keep as disabled item or drop. Recommendation: keep disabled, mirrors current intent and avoids missing-route handling.
- **fixtures/seed** — `server/utils/dev-data.ts` or similar seed file may need a `featured: true` row so devs see the new hero by default.

```
1	import {
2	  parseFilters,
3	  searchParamsToInput,
4	  serializeFilters,
5	  type Filters,
6	} from "~~/shared/validators/filters"
7	
8	export type FilterUpdate = Partial<Filters>
9	
10	export function useFilters() {
11	  const route = useRoute()
12	  const router = useRouter()
13	  const localePath = useLocalePath()
14	
15	  const filters = computed<Filters>(() => {
16	    const params = new URLSearchParams()
17	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
18	      if (Array.isArray(v)) {
19	        for (const item of v) if (item !== null) params.append(k, String(item))
20	      } else if (v !== null && v !== undefined) {
21	        params.append(k, String(v))
22	      }
23	    }
24	    return parseFilters(searchParamsToInput(params))
25	  })
26	
27	  function update(partial: FilterUpdate) {
28	    const next: FilterUpdate = { ...filters.value, ...partial }
29	    if (!("page" in partial)) next.page = undefined
30	    const params = serializeFilters(next)
31	    const qs = params.toString()
32	    const path = route.path
33	    router.replace(qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path)
34	  }
35	
36	  function hrefForFilters(partial: FilterUpdate, basePath = "/extensions") {
37	    const next: FilterUpdate = { ...filters.value, ...partial }
38	    if (!("page" in partial)) next.page = undefined
39	    const params = serializeFilters(next)
40	    const qs = params.toString()
41	    const path = localePath(basePath)
42	    return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path
43	  }
44	
45	  return { filters, update, hrefForFilters }
46	}
47

## Approved Plan:
# Elegance refactor — phased layout pass

## Context

The site has a solid editorial design system (Fraunces + Inter + JetBrains Mono, three themes, semantic CSS variables) but reads as "utility chrome" because the top bar carries nine affordances, the home hero ships an empty gradient with the literal word "AgentCenter" as payload, the sidebar runs three competing sections (Browse + 3-level Categories + Collections) in a 240px rail, and the accent appears 113 times across 61 files. Goal: keep the look-and-feel system, raise the elegance ceiling by reducing chrome, giving the home a real editorial moment, and dialling back accent saturation.

User selected **all four moves as a phased program**, constraint: **stay within the current themes** (no new color tokens, no new fonts; lucide icons are not tokens and are fine to add).

## Locked decisions for this refactor

1. Phase order: **B → A → C → D.** B carries the only irreversible move (schema/migration), so it lands first and stands alone. A unblocks the mcp-panorama nav strand. C deletes UI A already provided cover for. D is the lowest-risk tail and only makes sense after the surfaces it tints stabilize.
2. **mcp-panorama nav strand**: drop the forced `sidebarCollapsed = true` in `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue:6`. The page's own `LayerSidebar` renders inside `<main>`, not in the global aside slot, so they don't conflict. Cheapest correct answer; no new Sheet or icon-rail.
3. **Hand-curated `featured` flag**, not derived. A boolean column on `extensions`. Composes cleanly with the existing `badge` enum (`official` / `popular` / `new`). The existing `findFeatured()` is rewritten to prefer the flag, falling back to top-downloaded so the home is never empty.
4. **Sidebar after refactor**: primary nav (top) → Browse (type pills) → Collections (placeholders). No taxonomy tree.
5. **Accent convention** (recorded in `CLAUDE.md` Locked Product Decisions as item 11): accent reserved for primary CTAs, primary filter active state (ScopePills), focus rings, and featured editorial moments. Secondary active states use neutral ink + left-rule, not tinted fills.
6. **Delivery**: single feature branch, **one PR, 8 commits** in order B → A → C → D. This consciously overrides the CLAUDE.md "pause at each phase boundary for a human checkpoint" rule for this refactor — the user opted for one review cycle over four. Trade-off accepted: Phase B's schema migration ships in the same PR as the UI work, so it can't be reverted independently if A/C/D need rework.

## Phase B — Featured home hero (lands first)

**Why first**: schema change is the only irreversible move; isolate it.

Files touched:
- `shared/db/schema/extension.ts` — add `featured: boolean().notNull().default(false)` after `visibility` (around line 93). Composite index `(featured, publishedAt DESC)` for the lookup.
- `drizzle/` — generate migration via `bun drizzle-kit generate`. `default false` is safe on populated tables.
- `server/repositories/extensions.ts` — extend `listSelect` (lines 24–43) to include `tagline` + `taglineZh` (currently only in `detailSelect`, lines 59–60). Rewrite `findFeatured` (lines 121–131) to `where featured = true and visibility = 'published' order by published_at desc limit 1`, with a fallback select to top-downloaded when no row is flagged. Returns a single `ExtensionListItem | null`.
- `server/api/internal/featured.get.ts` — new endpoint, thin wrapper returning `{ featured }`.
- `app/components/extension/ExtFeatureHero.vue` — new component. Reuses `ExtHero.vue` typography rhythm and `InstallCommand.vue` for the install line; eyebrow from existing i18n key `home.featuredLabel` ("Featured this week"). Localizes name/description/tagline via the `*Zh` columns the same way `ExtCard.vue:20-27` already does.
- `app/pages/index.vue` — replace lines 26–45 with `<ExtFeatureHero v-if="featured" :extension="featured" />` plus a slim fallback hero for the empty case. Second `useFetch('/api/internal/featured')`.

Reused: `ExtHero` typography, `InstallCommand`, `home.featuredLabel`, the `nameZh`/`descriptionZh`/`taglineZh` i18n column pattern.
Created: schema column, migration, endpoint, `ExtFeatureHero.vue`, possibly one seed row in dev fixtures so devs see the new hero on first run.

- `tagline` is nullable — hero must handle null gracefully (omit, don't show empty paragraph).

- New `server/repositories/extensions.featured.test.ts` (integration) — featured row wins over top-downloaded; fallback path returns top-downloaded when no flag.

Verification: `bun run db:migrate && bun run dev`; home renders fallback; `UPDATE extensions SET featured = true WHERE slug = '...';` → home shows the new hero across all three themes.

Commits: `feat(p?-db): add featured column to extensions` + migration; `feat(p?-repo): findFeatured prefers featured rows`; `feat(p?-ui): ExtFeatureHero on home`.

## Phase A — Top bar slim-down + sidebar primary nav

- `app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue:22` — header `h-[52px]` → `h-[64px]` to match.

Created: none.

`useFilters` is already generic — it round-trips every key in the `Filters` schema, including `funcCat`/`subCat`/`l2` (`shared/validators/filters.ts:16-18`). No validator changes needed. The new picker calls `update({ funcCat, subCat, l2 })` and the existing composable does the URL work.

- `app/components/filters/FuncCategoryPicker.vue` — new component, mirroring `app/components/filters/DeptPicker.vue`'s `expandedPath` + `flatten()` + Popover pattern (DeptPicker is the cleanest existing analogue because departments are also dotted-hierarchical). - `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — slot `<FuncCategoryPicker />` next to `<DeptPicker />`.

Reused: Popover primitives, `useFilters`, `FUNC_TAXONOMY`, `FUNC_CAT_COLORS`, DeptPicker's expansion-state pattern (`expandedPath` lines 25–40, `flatten()` lines 56–63).
Created: one new picker component.

URL contract: identical to today's sidebar — `?funcCat=workTask&subCat=systemDesign&l2=performance` round-trips without change so any shared/saved links keep working.

## Phase D — Accent restraint

- `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` — active-state swap on remaining call sites (after C, three left: primary-nav, Browse items, Collections): `bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent)` → `border-l-2 border-(--color-accent) pl-[6px] text-(--color-ink) font-semibold`.
- `app/components/filters/DeptPicker.vue`, the new `FuncCategoryPicker.vue`, `CreatorPicker.vue`, `PublisherPicker.vue`, `FilterChips.vue` — secondary pickers use neutral active style (`border-(--color-ink) text-(--color-ink) font-medium`). `ScopePills.vue` keeps `bg-(--color-accent)/10` as the primary filter — single accent moment in the filter row.
- `app/pages/index.vue` — remove the `from-(--color-accent)/10` gradient on the *fallback* hero block. The featured hero is the home's one accent moment.
- `CLAUDE.md` — add item 11 to Locked Product Decisions: accent convention (see Locked decision #5 above).
- `docs/plan.md` — record same convention in the UI section.

Untouched (intentional):

- `app/pages/sign-in.vue`, `sign-up.vue`, `cli/auth.vue`, `onboard.vue`, `error.vue` — CTA-heavy by design.

Verification: visual pass through home / extensions list / detail / publish / profile in all three themes; count accent hits with ripgrep before and after.

89	export async function findManyForList(
90	  db: Transactable,
91	  filters: Filters,
92	  userDeptId?: string,
93	) {
94	  const where = buildExtensionWhere(db, filters, userDeptId)
95	  const order = buildExtensionOrder(filters.sort)
96	
97	  return db
98	    .select(listSelect)
99	    .from(extensions)
100	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
101	    .where(where)
102	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
103	    .orderBy(...order)
104	    .limit(PAGE_SIZE)
105	    .offset(pageOffset(filters.page))
106	}
107	
108	export async function countFiltered(
109	  db: Transactable,
110	  filters: Filters,
111	  userDeptId?: string,
112	): Promise<number> {
113	  const where = buildExtensionWhere(db, filters, userDeptId)
114	  const [row] = await db
115	    .select({ count: count() })
116	    .from(extensions)
117	    .where(where)
118	  return Number(row?.count ?? 0)
119	}
120	
121	export async function findFeatured(db: Transactable) {
122	  const [row] = await db
123	    .select(listSelect)
124	    .from(extensions)
125	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
126	    .where(eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))
127	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
128	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))
129	    .limit(1)
130	  return row ?? null
131	}
132	
133	export async function findRelated(
134	  db: Transactable,
135	  extensionId: string,
136	  category: "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins",
137	) {
138	  return db
139	    .select(relatedSelect)
140	    .from(extensions)
141	    .where(
142	      and(
143	        eq(extensions.category, category),
144	        eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
145	        ne(extensions.id, extensionId),
146	      ),
147	    )
148	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))
149	    .limit(4)
150	}
151	
152	export async function findBySlug(
153	  db: Transactable,
154	  slug: string,
155	  opts: { onlyPublished?: boolean } = {},
156	) {
157	  const onlyPublished = opts.onlyPublished ?? true
158	  const where = onlyPublished
159	    ? and(eq(extensions.slug, slug), eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))
160	    : eq(extensions.slug, slug)
161	
162	  const [row] = await db
163	    .select(detailSelect)
164	    .from(extensions)
165	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
166	    .where(where)
167	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
168	  return row ?? null
169	}
170	
171	export async function findById(db: Transactable, id: string) {
172	  const [row] = await db
173	    .select(detailSelect)
174	    .from(extensions)
175	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
176	    .where(eq(extensions.id, id))
177	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
178	  return row ?? null
179	}
180	
181	export interface VisibilityUpdate {
182	  visibility: "draft" | "published" | "archived"
183	  publishedAt: Date | null
184	}
185	
186	export async function updateVisibility(
187	  db: Transactable,
188	  id: string,
189	  payload: VisibilityUpdate,
190	): Promise<void> {
191	  await db.update(extensions).set(payload).where(eq(extensions.id, id))
192	}
193	
194	export async function incrementDownloads(
195	  db: Transactable,
196	  id: string,
197	): Promise<void> {
198	  await db
199	    .update(extensions)
200	    .set({ downloadsCount: sql`${extensions.downloadsCount} + 1` })
201	    .where(eq(extensions.id, id))
202	}
203	
```

20	// tsvector is a Postgres-native type; Drizzle doesn't ship a built-in helper.
21	// We declare it as a custom type so the ORM can reference it in WHERE clauses
22	// via sql``. It is GENERATED ALWAYS (read-only); never written by app code.
23	const tsvector = customType<{ data: string }>({
24	  dataType() { return "tsvector"; },
25	});
26	
27	export const extensionCategoryEnum = pgEnum("extension_category", [
28	  "skills",
29	  "mcp",
30	  "slash",
31	  "plugins",
32	]);

34	export const extensionScopeEnum = pgEnum("extension_scope", [
35	  "personal",

37	  "enterprise",
38	]);
39	
40	export const extensionBadgeEnum = pgEnum("extension_badge", [
41	  "official",

43	  "new",
44	]);
45	
46	export const funcCatEnum = pgEnum("func_cat", [
47	  "workTask",
48	  "business",
49	  "tools",
50	]);
51	
52	export const visibilityEnum = pgEnum("extension_visibility", [
53	  "draft",
54	  "published",
55	  "archived",
56	]);
57	
58	export const versionStatusEnum = pgEnum("version_status", [
59	  "pending",
60	  "scanning",
61	  "ready",
62	  "rejected",
63	]);
64	
65	export const fileScanStatusEnum = pgEnum("file_scan_status", [
66	  "pending",
67	  "clean",
68	  "flagged",
69	]);
70	
71	export const extensions = pgTable(
72	  "extensions",
73	  {
74	    id: text().primaryKey(),
75	    slug: text().notNull().unique(),
76	    category: extensionCategoryEnum().notNull(),
77	    badge: extensionBadgeEnum(),
78	    scope: extensionScopeEnum().notNull(),
79	    // funcCat/subCat are nullable — the redesigned publish wizard does not
80	    // collect them; admin curation can backfill or system defaults apply.
81	    funcCat: funcCatEnum(),
82	    subCat: text(),
83	    l2: text(),
84	    publisherUserId: text().references(() => users.id, {
85	      onDelete: "set null",
86	    }),
87	    ownerOrgId: text()
88	      .notNull()
89	      .references(() => organizations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
90	    deptId: text().references(() => departments.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
91	    iconEmoji: text(),
92	    iconColor: text(),
93	    visibility: visibilityEnum().notNull().default("draft"),
94	    // i18n columns
95	    name: text().notNull(),
96	    nameZh: text(),
97	    tagline: text(),
98	    taglineZh: text(),
99	    description: text(),
100	    descriptionZh: text(),
101	    // Long-form markdown body for the detail page. Not duplicated to ZH yet —
102	    // most extensions will have an English README, with translations layered
103	    // on later if needed.
104	    readmeMd: text(),
105	    // metadata for detail page sidebar
106	    homepageUrl: text(),
107	    repoUrl: text(),
108	    licenseSpdx: text(),
109	    compatibilityJson: jsonb(),
110	    // Permissions captured during publish (network/files/runtime/data, etc.).
111	    // Surfaced on the detail page so users see what the extension wants.
112	    permissions: jsonb().notNull().default({}),
113	    // denormalized counters (updated by jobs)
114	    downloadsCount: integer().notNull().default(0),
115	    starsAvg: numeric({ precision: 2, scale: 1 }).notNull().default("0.0"),
116	    ratingsCount: integer().notNull().default(0),
117	    publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
118	    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
119	    updatedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
120	    searchVector: tsvector("search_vector"),
121	  },
122	  (t) => [
123	    index("idx_ext_category").on(t.category),
124	    index("idx_ext_scope").on(t.scope),
125	    index("idx_ext_func_sub_l2").on(t.funcCat, t.subCat, t.l2),
126	    index("idx_ext_dept_path").using(
127	      "btree",
128	      sql`${t.deptId} text_pattern_ops`,
129	    ),
130	    index("idx_ext_visibility").on(t.visibility),
131	    index("idx_ext_downloads").on(sql`${t.downloadsCount} DESC`),
132	    index("idx_ext_stars").on(sql`${t.starsAvg} DESC`),
133	  ],
134	);
135	
136	export const extensionVersions = pgTable(
137	  "extension_versions",
138	  {
139	    id: text().primaryKey(),
140	    extensionId: text()
141	      .notNull()
142	      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
143	    version: text().notNull(), // semver
144	    changelog: text(),
145	    changelogZh: text(),
146	    manifestJson: jsonb(),
147	    // Logical reference to files.id; no FK constraint to avoid a circular FK
148	    // with files.extensionVersionId. App code keeps these consistent.
149	    bundleFileId: text(),
150	    status: versionStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),
151	    // Source method chosen during publish: zip (only one wired today), git, cli.
152	    // Stored as text rather than enum so adding methods doesn't require a migration.
153	    sourceMethod: text().notNull().default("zip"),
154	    sourceMeta: jsonb().notNull().default({}),
155	    publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
156	    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
157	  },
158	  (t) => [unique("ext_version_unique").on(t.extensionId, t.version)],
159	);
160	
161	export const files = pgTable("files", {
162	  id: text().primaryKey(),
163	  extensionVersionId: text().references(() => extensionVersions.id, {
164	    onDelete: "set null",
165	  }),
166	  r2Key: text().notNull(),
167	  size: bigint({ mode: "bigint" }).notNull(),
168	  checksumSha256: text().notNull(),
169	  mimeType: text(),
170	  scanStatus: fileScanStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),
171	  scanReport: jsonb(),
172	  createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
173	});
174	
175	export const tags = pgTable("tags", {
176	  id: text().primaryKey(), // tag key, e.g. "real-time"
177	  labelEn: text().notNull(),
178	  labelZh: text().notNull(),
179	});
180	
181	export const extensionTags = pgTable(
182	  "extension_tags",
183	  {
184	    extensionId: text()
185	      .notNull()
186	      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
187	    tagId: text()
188	      .notNull()
189	      .references(() => tags.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
190	  },
191	  (t) => [
192	    primaryKey({ columns: [t.extensionId, t.tagId] }),
193	    index("idx_extension_tags_tag_ext").on(t.tagId, t.extensionId),
194	  ],
195	);

---
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { resolve } from "node:path"

import { defineConfig } from "drizzle-kit"

const envPath = resolve(".env")
if (existsSync(envPath)) {
  const content = readFileSync(envPath, "utf-8")
  for (const line of content.split("\n")) {
    const match = line.match(/^([A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*)=(.*)$/)
    if (match && !process.env[match[1]]) {
      process.env[match[1]] = match[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "")
    }
  }
}

const databaseUrl = process.env.DATABASE_URL
if (!databaseUrl) {
  throw new Error(
    "Missing DATABASE_URL env var required for Drizzle Kit. " +
      "Set it in .env or your shell before running db:* scripts.",
  )
}

export default defineConfig({
  schema: "./shared/db/schema/index.ts",
  out: "./drizzle",
  dialect: "postgresql",
  dbCredentials: { url: databaseUrl },
  casing: "snake_case",
  verbose: true,
  strict: true,
})
```

402	## 10. Milestone order
403	
404	| Phase | Duration | Deliverable |
405	|---|---|---|
406	| **0. Bootstrap** | 0.5d | `bunx nuxi init`, Nuxt 4 compat, Tailwind v4 via `@nuxtjs/tailwindcss` (or `@tailwindcss/vite`), shadcn-vue init, `@nuxt/fonts`, `@nuxtjs/i18n` skeleton, `@nuxt/eslint`, `@pinia/nuxt`, Vitest + `@nuxt/test-utils`, Playwright. CI workflow (`bun run validate`). Adapted `CLAUDE.md` + this plan committed. |
407	| **1. Visual shell** | 1.5d | `app.vue` + `default` layout, `TopBar` + `Sidebar`, theme tokens (Ivory + Dark) in `tailwind.css`, `ThemeSwitch` (cookie-backed, no flash on hydrate), `LocaleSwitch`. Static home pixel-matched to the prototype, statically renders. |
408	| **2. DB + seed** | 1d | Port `shared/db/schema/*` from the original. Copy `drizzle/` migrations. `server/utils/db.ts` (postgres-js + Drizzle). `scripts/seed.ts` ported (Bun runnable). Working `useAsyncData` query on the home page. |
409	| **3. Browse + filter (vertical-slice demo)** | 2d | `/[locale]/extensions/index.vue` with all filters wired through query string; `useFilters` composable; `ExtCard` + `ExtGrid`; `FilterBar` Mode B; `DeptPicker` popover; `SortSelect`. Home shows featured banner + first 6 + Browse All. **Anonymous browse works.** ← clickable demo URL by end of this phase |
410	| **4. Detail page** | 1d | `/[locale]/extensions/[slug].vue` — `Markdown.vue` (markdown-it + DOMPurify), metadata sidebar (homepage/repo/license/compat), version table, install button (placeholder, wired in Phase 9). Port `getExtensionBySlug`, `listExtensionVersions`, `getRelatedExtensions`. `ExtTabs.vue` over `reka-ui` tabs primitive. |
411	| **5. i18n** | 1d | `@nuxtjs/i18n` wired, `en` + `zh` messages from prototype's `T`, locale switcher, locale-redirect middleware, always-prefixed URLs. |
412	| **6. Auth** | 1.5d | Better Auth + Drizzle adapter, server handler at `server/api/auth/[...].ts`, sign-in/up pages, dept-pick onboarding, `getSessionUser()` / `requireUser()` helpers, protect publish/collection routes via named middleware. |
413	| **7. Public registry API + manifest spec** | 1d | `server/api/v1/extensions/...` endpoints with frozen JSON shapes, signed bundle redirect, contract tests (Vitest). Port `docs/manifest-spec.md` from the original. |
414	| **8. Storage + upload signing** | 0.5d | `server/utils/storage.ts` with Supabase Storage default; `server/api/upload/sign.post.ts`; `scripts/set-storage-cors.ts`. R2 alternative documented in `docs/deploy.md`. |
415	| **9. Collections + Install events** | 1d | `server/api/internal/collections/...`, `server/api/internal/installs.post.ts`, system 'installed' / 'saved' collections seeded per user on sign-up. Port `shared/installs/record.ts`. Web install button wired. |
416	| **10. Upload wizard** | 2d | `UploadWizard.vue` 4-step (Basics → Bundle → Listing → Review) with vee-validate + Zod (same schemas as original), presigned upload, `internal/publish/*` endpoints. Resume + discard flows. `DiscardButton` calls `internal/publish/discard`. |
417	| **11. Background jobs** | 1d | Inngest client + `scan-bundle` + `reindex-search` functions, webhook route at `server/api/inngest.post.ts`, status polling on publisher dashboard. Port `shared/extensions/state.ts`. |
418	| **12. Search FTS** | 0.5d | Apply `0002_fts_search_vector.sql` to dev DB, trgm indexes, top-bar search wired through `useFilters`. |
419	| **13. Polish** | 1.5d | Empty states, `error.vue` route-level boundaries, loading skeletons via `<Suspense>` + skeleton components, OG images via `@nuxtjs/og-image`, a11y pass, dark-mode polish across all surfaces. |
420	| **14. Deploy** | 1d | Node preset (`nitro: { preset: 'node-server' }`), env template, deploy runbook in `docs/deploy.md`, smoke test the CLI against the new URL (with `cli/src/config-store.ts` pointed at it). |
421	
422	**Total: ~17 days** to v1. Clickable demo URL by end of Phase 3 (~5 days in).
423	
424	Each phase ends with a commit on its branch and a PR back to `main`. CI mirrors `bun run validate` (see §11). Tag the merge commit (`p0`, `p1`, …) to make per-phase rollback trivial.
425	
426	### Status (2026-05-13)
427	
428	- **P0 – P13: shipped** in the original rewrite track (#1 – #9).
429	- **P14 (Deploy): pending.** The Node preset is already configured in `nuxt.config.ts`; the runbook in `docs/deploy.md` is up to date; what's left is wiring the production environment and flipping the CLI's default registry to point at it.
430	- **P14a – P20: rewrite-completion track, all merged.** A second pass closed gaps the audit found at the end of the initial rewrite:
431	  - **P14a** — trim `CLAUDE.md` to short-form, point to this plan for details (#10).
432	  - **P14** — import `cli/` as a standalone Bun project; ship a cross-platform Node JS bundle (#10).
433	  - **P15** — port shadcn-vue primitives backed by reka-ui (#10).
434	  - **P16** — filter drawer pickers: DeptPicker, TagDrawer, CreatorPicker, PublisherPicker + facets endpoint (#11).
435	  - **P17** — 4-step publish wizard with live preview + `publish/[id]/edit` resume flow (#11).
436	  - **P18** — `/profile` My Workspace page with 9 components + 3 internal endpoints (#12).
437	  - **P19** — split the detail page into hero / about / tabs / related + InstallCommand / ShareButton / SaveButton (#12).
438	  - **P20** — `nuxt-og-image` for social previews + Playwright browse / detail / navigation specs (#12).
439	
440	The "P14" label is reused intentionally — the original P14 (Deploy) never executed before the rewrite-completion track began, so P14a was named to avoid clashing with the unfinished P14 deploy work.
441	
442	### P1 (post-v1)
443	
444	- Publisher dashboard improvements (install metrics, version diffs)
445	- Collection sharing
446	- Ratings & reviews UI on detail page
447	- View events + trending calculation
448	- CLI installers for MCP / slash commands / plugins (in the carried-over CLI repo)
449	
450	### P2
451	
452	- MDX docs site at `/docs`
453	- SSO / SAML for enterprise sign-up
454	- Multi-tenant UI (org switcher, per-org branding)
455	- Meilisearch swap-in (only if Postgres FTS is hitting limits)
456	
457	---
458	
459	## 11. Validate pipeline
460	
461	`bun run validate` is the primary command — the fast inner loop that mirrors the first four steps of CI. It chains four steps in order, failing fast. The fifth gate, `bun run test:integration`, runs PGlite-backed repository tests; CI runs it on every PR but it's not part of `validate` (kept separate so the local typecheck/test loop stays under a few seconds). Phase 0 landed a working `bun run validate` against the bare scaffold; every subsequent phase keeps it green.

2	
3	import * as extensionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/extensions"
4	import { extensions, extensionTags, organizations, tags } from "~~/shared/db/schema"

7	import { setupDb, type TestDb } from "../helpers/db"
8	
9	describe("extensions repository", () => {
10	  let db: TestDb
11	  let cleanup: () => Promise<void>
12	
13	  beforeAll(async () => {
14	    const handle = await setupDb()
15	    db = handle.db
16	    cleanup = handle.cleanup
17	
18	    await db.insert(organizations).values({
19	      id: "org-1",
20	      slug: "org-1",
21	      name: "Org One",
22	    })
23	    await db.insert(tags).values([
24	      { id: "automation", labelEn: "Automation", labelZh: "自动化" },
25	      { id: "k8s", labelEn: "Kubernetes", labelZh: "K8s" },
26	    ])
27	  })
28	
29	  afterAll(async () => {
30	    await cleanup()
31	  })
32	
33	  beforeEach(async () => {
34	    // Each test gets a clean extensions table so list assertions are
35	    // independent. We don't need to clear orgs/tags — those are stable seed.
36	    await db.delete(extensionTags)
37	    await db.delete(extensions)
38	  })
39	
40	  async function seedExtension(overrides: Partial<typeof extensions.$inferInsert> = {}) {
41	    const row: typeof extensions.$inferInsert = {
42	      id: "ext-1",
43	      slug: "kubernetes-helper",
44	      category: "skills",
45	      scope: "personal",
46	      ownerOrgId: "org-1",
47	      name: "Kubernetes Helper",
48	      description: "A helper for kubernetes",
49	      visibility: "published",
50	      downloadsCount: 10,
51	      ...overrides,
52	    }
53	    await db.insert(extensions).values(row)
54	    return row.id!
55	  }
56	
57	  describe("findBySlug", () => {
58	    it("returns a published extension by slug with tag ids aggregated", async () => {
59	      await seedExtension()
60	      await db.insert(extensionTags).values([
61	        { extensionId: "ext-1", tagId: "automation" },
62	        { extensionId: "ext-1", tagId: "k8s" },
63	      ])
64	
65	      const ext = await extensionsRepo.findBySlug(db, "kubernetes-helper")
66	      expect(ext).not.toBeNull()
67	      expect(ext!.slug).toBe("kubernetes-helper")
68	      expect([...(ext!.tagIds ?? [])].sort()).toEqual(["automation", "k8s"])
69	    })
70	
71	    it("returns null for a draft extension when onlyPublished is implicit (true)", async () => {
72	      await seedExtension({ visibility: "draft" })
73	      const ext = await extensionsRepo.findBySlug(db, "kubernetes-helper")
74	      expect(ext).toBeNull()
75	    })
76	
77	    it("returns a draft extension when onlyPublished is false", async () => {
78	      await seedExtension({ visibility: "draft" })
79	      const ext = await extensionsRepo.findBySlug(
80	        db,
81	        "kubernetes-helper",
82	        { onlyPublished: false },
83	      )
84	      expect(ext).not.toBeNull()
85	      expect(ext!.visibility).toBe("draft")
86	    })
87	
88	    it("returns null when no extension matches", async () => {
89	      const ext = await extensionsRepo.findBySlug(db, "nonexistent")
90	      expect(ext).toBeNull()
91	    })
92	  })
93	
94	  describe("findById", () => {
95	    it("returns an extension by id regardless of visibility", async () => {
96	      await seedExtension({ visibility: "draft" })
97	      const ext = await extensionsRepo.findById(db, "ext-1")
98	      expect(ext?.id).toBe("ext-1")
99	    })
100	
101	    it("returns null for an unknown id", async () => {
102	      const ext = await extensionsRepo.findById(db, "nope")
103	      expect(ext).toBeNull()
104	    })
105	  })
106	
107	  describe("findManyForList + countFiltered", () => {
108	    it("returns only published extensions and counts them", async () => {
109	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-1", slug: "a", visibility: "published" })
110	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-2", slug: "b", visibility: "published" })
111	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-3", slug: "c", visibility: "draft" })
112	
113	      const filters = parseFilters({ sort: "recent", dept: "__all" })
114	      const rows = await extensionsRepo.findManyForList(db, filters)
115	      const total = await extensionsRepo.countFiltered(db, filters)
116	      expect(rows.map((r) => r.slug).sort()).toEqual(["a", "b"])
117	      expect(total).toBe(2)

120	
121	  describe("findFeatured", () => {

123	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-low", slug: "low", downloadsCount: 5 })
124	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-high", slug: "high", downloadsCount: 50 })
125	      await seedExtension({

127	        slug: "draft",
128	        downloadsCount: 999,
129	        visibility: "draft",
130	      })
131	
132	      const featured = await extensionsRepo.findFeatured(db)
133	      expect(featured?.slug).toBe("high")

136	    it("returns null when nothing is published", async () => {

138	      const featured = await extensionsRepo.findFeatured(db)
139	      expect(featured).toBeNull()

142	
143	  describe("findRelated", () => {
144	    it("returns up to 4 published extensions of the same category, excluding self", async () => {
145	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-1", slug: "self", category: "skills" })
146	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-2", slug: "p1", category: "skills", downloadsCount: 30 })
147	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-3", slug: "p2", category: "skills", downloadsCount: 20 })
148	      await seedExtension({ id: "ext-4", slug: "mcp1", category: "mcp" })
149	      await seedExtension({

151	        slug: "draft1",
152	        category: "skills",
153	        visibility: "draft",
154	      })
155	
156	      const related = await extensionsRepo.findRelated(db, "ext-1", "skills")
157	      const slugs = related.map((r) => r.slug)
158	      expect(slugs).toEqual(["p1", "p2"])
159	    })
160	  })
161	
162	  describe("updateVisibility", () => {
163	    it("flips a draft to published with publishedAt set", async () => {
164	      await seedExtension({ visibility: "draft" })
165	      const now = new Date("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z")
166	      await extensionsRepo.updateVisibility(db, "ext-1", {
167	        visibility: "published",
168	        publishedAt: now,
169	      })
170	      const ext = await extensionsRepo.findById(db, "ext-1")
171	      expect(ext?.visibility).toBe("published")
172	      expect(ext?.publishedAt?.toISOString()).toBe(now.toISOString())
173	    })
174	  })
175	
176	  describe("incrementDownloads", () => {
177	    it("adds 1 to downloads_count atomically — concurrent calls don't lose updates", async () => {
178	      await seedExtension({ downloadsCount: 0 })
179	      // SQL-level `+ 1` is what makes this safe under contention; the
180	      // assertion would fail if the function were implemented as
181	      // "read, increment in JS, write" rather than `set ... = ... + 1`.
182	      await Promise.all(
183	        Array.from({ length: 20 }, () =>
184	          extensionsRepo.incrementDownloads(db, "ext-1"),
185	        ),
186	      )
187	      const ext = await extensionsRepo.findById(db, "ext-1")
188	      expect(ext?.downloadsCount).toBe(20)
189	    })
190	  })
191	})

  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/repositories/extensions.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function findFeatured(db: Transactable) {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select(listSelect)\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))\n    .where(eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"))\n    .groupBy(extensions.id)\n    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))\n    .limit(1)\n  return row ?? null\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function findFeatured(db: Transactable) {\n  // Prefer the hand-curated row. Fall back to top-downloaded so the home\n  // hero is never empty.\n  const [curated] = await db\n    .select(listSelect)\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"),\n        eq(extensions.featured, true),\n      ),\n    )\n    .groupBy(extensions.id)\n    .orderBy(desc(extensions.publishedAt))\n    .limit(1)\n  if (curated) return curated\n\n  const [fallback] = await db\n    .select(listSelect)\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))\n    .where(eq(extensions.visibility, \"published\"))\n    .groupBy(extensions.id)\n    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))\n    .limit(1)\n  return fallback ?? null\n}"
}
```

import * as extensionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/extensions"
import {
  PAGE_SIZE,
  parseFilters,
  searchParamsToInput,
} from "~~/shared/validators/filters"

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
  const url = getRequestURL(event)
  const filters = parseFilters({
    ...searchParamsToInput(url.searchParams),
    dept: "__all",
  })

  try {
    const db = useDb()
    const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
      extensionsRepo.findManyForList(db, filters),
      extensionsRepo.countFiltered(db, filters),
    ])

    setHeader(
      event,
      "Cache-Control",
      "public, s-maxage=60, stale-while-revalidate=300",
    )

    return {
      items: items.map((ext) => ({
        slug: ext.slug,
        name: ext.name,
        nameZh: ext.nameZh,
        category: ext.category,
        scope: ext.scope,
        badge: ext.badge,
        description: ext.description,
        descriptionZh: ext.descriptionZh,
        tags: ext.tagIds,
        funcCat: ext.funcCat,
        subCat: ext.subCat,
```

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Check, Copy } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	
4	const props = defineProps<{ command: string }>()
5	
6	const { t } = useI18n()
7	const copied = ref(false)
8	let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
9	
10	async function copy() {
11	  if (!navigator.clipboard) return
12	  try {
13	    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(props.command)
14	    copied.value = true
15	    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
16	    timer = setTimeout(() => { copied.value = false }, 1400)
17	  } catch {
18	    // ignore — clipboard permissions vary
19	  }
20	}
21	
22	onBeforeUnmount(() => { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer) })
23	</script>
24	
25	<template>
26	  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-(--color-ink) text-(--color-bg) p-3 font-mono text-sm">
27	    <span class="flex-1 truncate">$ {{ command }}</span>
28	    <button
29	      type="button"
30	      class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs text-(--color-bg)/80 hover:text-(--color-bg)"
31	      :aria-label="copied ? t('extensions.copied') : t('extensions.copy')"
32	      @click="copy"
33	    >
34	      <component :is="copied ? Check : Copy" :size="12" aria-hidden="true" />
35	      {{ copied ? t("extensions.copied") : t("extensions.copy") }}
36	    </button>
37	  </div>
38	</template>
39

21	  }
22	})
23	
24	// Decorative motif: a fixed 6×4 grid (6 columns, 4 rows) of cells using the
25	// three status tokens at low opacity. Deterministic, no randomness.
26	const motif = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) => {
27	  const r = (i * 7 + 3) % 11
28	  if (r < 5) return "released"

31	})

---
import * as extensionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/extensions"
import {
  parseFilters,
  searchParamsToInput,
} from "~~/shared/validators/filters"

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
  const url = getRequestURL(event)
  const filters = parseFilters(searchParamsToInput(url.searchParams))

  try {
    const db = useDb()
    const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
      extensionsRepo.findManyForList(db, filters),
      extensionsRepo.countFiltered(db, filters),
    ])
    return { items, total, filters }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[api/internal/extensions] db error:", err)
    throw createError({
      statusCode: 500,
      statusMessage: "Failed to load extensions",
    })
  }
})
```

  "content": "import * as extensionsRepo from \"~~/server/repositories/extensions\"\n\nexport default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  try {\n    const db = useDb()\n    const featured = await extensionsRepo.findFeatured(db)\n    setHeader(\n      event,\n      \"Cache-Control\",\n      \"public, s-maxage=60, stale-while-revalidate=300\",\n    )\n    return { featured }\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[api/internal/featured] db error:\", err)\n    throw createError({\n      statusCode: 500,\n      statusMessage: \"Failed to load featured extension\",\n    })\n  }\n})\n"
}

 taglineZh: null,\n    description: \"Search the web straight from your agent — no copy-paste detour.\",\n    descriptionZh: null,\n    downloadsCount: 1248,\n    starsAvg: \"4.7\",\n    tagIds: [],\n 40	      props: { collapsed: true },
41	    })
42	    expect(wrapper.findAll("a").length).toBe(0)
43	  })

46	    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)
47	    // happy-dom strips/canonicalizes oklch() in style attributes — check via raw outerHTML
48	    const styled = wrapper.findAll("span").filter((s) => {
49	      const styleAttr = s.attributes("style")
50	      return typeof styleAttr === "string" && styleAttr.length > 0
51	    })
52	    expect(styled.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
53	  })
54	
55	  it("workTask is expanded by default, exposing 'System Design'", async () => {
56	    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)
57	    expect(wrapper.html().toLowerCase()).toContain("system design")
58	  })
59	
60	  it("a hidden category does not expose its l1 children", async () => {

63	  })

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nconst { t } = useI18n()\n// The MCP Panorama page renders its own LayerSidebar (which depends on\n// page state). The global aside ships collapsed by default so LayerSidebar\n// owns the gutter, but the TopBar menu toggle still opens the global nav\n// on demand — primary nav stays reachable from this page.\nconst sidebarCollapsed = ref(true)\nfunction toggleSidebar() {\n  sidebarCollapsed.value = !sidebarCollapsed.value\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"h-screen flex flex-col bg-(--color-bg) text-(--color-ink)\">\n    <a\n      href=\"#main-content\"\n      class=\"sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:absolute focus:top-2 focus:left-2 focus:z-50 focus:px-3 focus:py-2 focus:bg-(--color-card) focus:text-(--color-ink) focus:rounded-md focus:shadow\"\n    >\n      {{ t(\"nav.skipToContent\") }}\n    </a>\n\n    <header\n      class=\"sticky top-0 z-10 h-16 border-b border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) flex items-center px-5 gap-4\"\n      role=\"banner\"\n    >\n      <TopBar :collapsed=\"sidebarCollapsed\" @toggle-sidebar=\"toggleSidebar\" />\n    </header>\n\n    <div class=\"flex flex-1 min-h-0\">\n      <aside\n        class=\"border-r border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-sidebar) overflow-hidden flex flex-col transition-[width] duration-200\"\n        :class=\"sidebarCollapsed ? 'w-0' : 'w-[240px]'\"\n        role=\"navigation\"\n      >\n        <Sidebar :collapsed=\"sidebarCollapsed\" />\n      </aside>\n\n      <main id=\"main-content\" tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"flex flex-1 min-h-0 focus:outline-none\">\n        <slot />\n      </main>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

The search\ninput becomes a ghost field: borderless on a tinted background until\nfocus, where it surfaces a border + accent ring.\n\nHeader grows 52px → 64px (h-16) in both default and mcp-panorama\nlayouts. mcp-panorama gains the global aside slot, defaulting to\ncollapsed so the page's own LayerSidebar keeps the gutter — the menu\ntoggle opens it on demand, so primary nav stays reachable from the\npanorama page.\n\ni18n: new sidebar.explore key (en/zh); all four nav labels reuse the\nexisting nav.* keys. 1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	import type { Department, Locale } from "~~/shared/types"
4	import { DEPARTMENTS, MY_DEPT_ID, deptPath, findDept } from "~~/shared/data/departments"
5	
6	const { t, locale } = useI18n()
7	const { filters, update } = useFilters()
8	
9	const open = ref(false)
10	
11	const activeDept = computed<Department | null>(() => {
12	  const id = filters.value.dept
13	  if (!id) return null
14	  return findDept(id)
15	})
16	
17	const triggerLabel = computed(() => {
18	  if (!activeDept.value) return t("filters.dept.all")
19	  if (activeDept.value.id === MY_DEPT_ID) return t("filters.dept.mine")
20	  const segments = deptPath(activeDept.value.id, locale.value as Locale)
21	  return segments.join(" / ")
22	})
23	
24	// Path of dept IDs from the active dept up to (and including) the root.
25	const expandedPath = computed<Set<string>>(() => {
26	  const set = new Set<string>()
27	  const targetId = filters.value.dept ?? MY_DEPT_ID
28	  const parts = targetId.split(".")
29	  for (let i = 1; i <= parts.length; i++) {
30	    set.add(parts.slice(0, i).join("."))
31	  }
32	  return set
33	})
34	
35	// Local mutable expansion state — seeded from expandedPath, then user can override.
36	const expanded = ref<Set<string>>(new Set(expandedPath.value))
37	
38	watch(open, (isOpen) => {
39	  if (isOpen) expanded.value = new Set(expandedPath.value)
40	})
41	
42	function toggleExpand(id: string) {
43	  const next = new Set(expanded.value)
44	  if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id)
45	  else next.add(id)
46	  expanded.value = next
47	}
48	
49	function selectDept(id: string | undefined) {
50	  update({ dept: id })
51	  open.value = false
52	}
53	
54	const orgRootId = computed(() => MY_DEPT_ID.split(".")[0])
55	
56	function flatten(dept: Department, depth: number): Array<{ dept: Department; depth: number }> {
57	  const out: Array<{ dept: Department; depth: number }> = [{ dept, depth }]
58	  if (dept.children && expanded.value.has(dept.id)) {
59	    for (const child of dept.children) {
60	      out.push(...flatten(child, depth + 1))
61	    }
62	  }
63	  return out
64	}
65	
66	const visibleRows = computed(() =>
67	  DEPARTMENTS.flatMap((d) => flatten(d, 0)),
68	)
69	
70	function deptName(d: Department): string {
71	  return locale.value === "zh" ? d.nameZh : d.name
72	}
73	</script>
74	
75	<template>
76	  <Popover v-model:open="open">
77	    <PopoverTrigger
78	      :class="[
79	        'inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] transition-colors',
80	        activeDept
81	          ? 'border-(--color-accent)/40 bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent)'
82	          : 'border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)',
83	      ]"
84	    >
85	      <span class="font-semibold">{{ t("filters.dept.label") }}:</span>
86	      <span class="truncate max-w-[180px]">{{ triggerLabel }}</span>
87	      <ChevronDown :size="12" aria-hidden="true" />
88	    </PopoverTrigger>
89	
90	    <PopoverContent align="start" :class="'w-[360px] p-0'">
91	      <div class="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-(--color-border) px-3 py-2">
92	        <button
93	          type="button"
94	          class="rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
95	          :class="filters.dept === MY_DEPT_ID
96	            ? 'border-(--color-accent) bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent)'
97	            : 'border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'"
98	          @click="selectDept(MY_DEPT_ID)"
99	        >
100	          {{ t("filters.dept.mine") }}
101	        </button>
102	        <button
103	          type="button"
104	          class="rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
105	          :class="filters.dept === orgRootId
106	            ? 'border-(--color-accent) bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent)'
107	            : 'border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'"
108	          @click="selectDept(orgRootId)"
109	        >
110	          {{ t("filters.dept.org") }}
111	        </button>
112	        <button
113	          type="button"
114	          class="rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
115	          :class="!filters.dept
116	            ? 'border-(--color-accent) bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent)'
117	            : 'border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'"
118	          @click="selectDept(undefined)"
119	        >
120	          {{ t("filters.dept.all") }}
121	        </button>
122	      </div>
123	
124	      <div class="max-h-72 overflow-y-auto py-1">
125	        <div
126	          v-for="row in visibleRows"
127	          :key="row.dept.id"
128	          class="flex items-center"
129	          :style="{ paddingLeft: `${8 + row.depth * 14}px` }"
130	        >
131	          <button
132	            v-if="row.dept.children && row.dept.children.length"
133	            type="button"
134	            class="grid size-5 place-items-center rounded text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)"
135	            :aria-label="expanded.has(row.dept.id)
136	              ? t('filters.dept.collapse')
137	              : t('filters.dept.expand')"
138	            @click="toggleExpand(row.dept.id)"
139	          >
140	            <ChevronDown v-if="expanded.has(row.dept.id)" :size="12" aria-hidden="true" />
141	            <ChevronRight v-else :size="12" aria-hidden="true" />
142	          </button>
143	          <span v-else class="block size-5" />
144	          <button
145	            type="button"
146	            class="flex flex-1 items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-sm px-2 py-1 text-left text-sm hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)"
147	            :class="filters.dept === row.dept.id
148	              ? 'text-(--color-accent) font-medium'
149	              : 'text-(--color-ink)'"
150	            @click="selectDept(row.dept.id)"
151	          >
152	            <span class="truncate">{{ deptName(row.dept) }}</span>
153	            <span
154	              v-if="row.dept.id === MY_DEPT_ID"
155	              class="font-mono text-[10px] text-(--color-ink-muted) bg-(--color-sidebar) px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded"
156	            >
157	              {{ t("filters.dept.mineBadge") }}
158	            </span>
159	          </button>
160	        </div>
161	      </div>
162	    </PopoverContent>
163	  </Popover>
164	</template>
165
